# SRS revision ledger — Revisions 1–174 (2026-07-27 →)

Moved out of `docs/SRS.md` on 2026-09-25 (Owner decision, cleanup). Each entry is the Document Owner's decision as recorded at the time; the normative clauses in `docs/SRS.md` cite them by number. Historical — not loaded by default (CLAUDE.md).


**Revision 175 (Document Owner decision of 2026-09-26 — a temporary Production tier, outside Morocco, that holds no beneficiary data):** **BUILT.** The Moroccan VPS and the CNDP filing both take time; the association wants its public calendar and public educational content reachable at `bodouralamal.com` meanwhile, without inviting anyone to register.

**§1 — THE TIER, AND THE EXCEPTION IT NEEDS.** The existing OVH host (Strasbourg; 2 vCPU, 4 GB, 40 GB) stops being Staging and becomes **temporary Production** at `bodouralamal.com`; `staging.bodouralamal.com` is retired until the Moroccan host exists, at which point the OVH box returns to Staging and the Moroccan one takes the name. **BR-18 and §2.2 are suspended for this tier and this scope only**: it may hold the Owner's own account (R115's precedent) and the association's own public content and events, and **no beneficiary records, no مؤطِّرة records and no recordings**. The Owner records that she accepts responsibility for any registration that arrives and deletes it unprocessed. The arrangement ends when the Moroccan host is provisioned; the move is the recovery-point restore the platform already drills.

**§2 — THE WAY IN IS NOT OFFERED, AND IS NOT CLOSED.** «تسجيل الدخول» renders nowhere on the public chrome — header, mobile menu, landing hero all go through one shared control that asks the deployment. **Nothing else changes** (the Owner, explicitly): `/login` and `/register` stay public and unchanged, `/register` keeps its own «المتابعة عبر Google», `POST /registrations` is untouched, the OAuth flow, sessions and «تسجيل الخروج» behave exactly as documented, and the Owner signs in at `/api/v1/auth/google`. The flag is `GET /site-config` → `{ sign_in_offered }`, public and anonymous, carrying no personal data, setting no cookie and reading no session — driven by the host variable `SIGN_IN_OFFERED`; **absent means offered**, so every other tier behaves as it always has. Guarded (`check-signin-offered.sh`): a second, ungated link to the OAuth entry fails the build. `robots.txt` allows the site and declines to advertise `/login` and `/register`.

**§3 — WHAT THE TIER MAY PROMISE A READER, AND IT IS TRUE TODAY.** Verified rather than assumed: the access log records time, request id, method, status, bytes and timings — **no IP address, no URL, no user-agent**; the API never reads the request address; `AuditLog` has no address column; `error_log` is `emerg` only; a reader who does not sign in receives **no cookie**; fonts are self-hosted and the CSP is `default-src 'self'`, so the browser contacts no third party. **The IP anonymisation the Owner approved was therefore unnecessary — there is nothing to anonymise.** The published سياسة الخصوصية and شروط الاستعمال say exactly this, and say that registration is not open in this phase and that any request received is deleted without being processed — **not** that it is closed, which would not be true while the form works.

**§4 — WHAT THE SWITCH ACTUALLY CHANGES ON THE HOST.** Certificate covering `bodouralamal.com`, `www.` and (during the transition) `staging.`; `server_name`; `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` and `STORAGE_BASE_URL`; the Google redirect URI and origin (the Owner's own step); and the production overlay. **The production overlay carries no memory ceilings** — they live in the staging overlay and are what has kept this 4 GB box stable — so they move with the tier rather than being lost with it. The backup schedule and a first recovery point are installed as part of the switch, because this tier now holds the only copy of what the association publishes. **The Staging gate is gone while this lasts**: `develop` → CI → this host, with the pre-deploy dump as the rollback.

**Revision 174 (Document Owner decisions of 2026-09-25 — eight items on the redesigned interface):** **BUILT AND VERIFIED; NO SCHEMA OR ROUTE CHANGE.**

**§1 — «الكل» IS THE ONE WORD FOR «NO FILTER».** Every dropdown's «all» option reads «الكل» («use only الكل in تقويمي, الجدول الزمني and any page having a drop down having an element all in it»); the field's label says what it is all of. Twenty-seven catalogue values changed; scope VALUES that mean «every branch» («كل المقرات الحالية والمستقبلية», the teaching scope) are not filters and keep their wording.

**§2 — THE SIDEBAR SCROLLS WITH THE PAGE.** On a wide screen the portal sidebar is static and compact and the «إخفاء/إظهار أقسام الإدارة» toggle is gone («not practical … having to scroll both»); reverses the 2026-08-17 sticky-with-its-own-scroll rule (UX rule W). On a phone the drawer and its opener stay.

**§3 — «المحتوى التعليمي» SHOWS EVERYTHING AT ONCE.** No Category page first: the library renders every visible item grouped Category → Level (the Category's own «كل مستويات الفئة» shelf first) → academic year → branch («بدون فرع» first) → Subject, with filters on each of those axes plus type and search. `?level=`/`?category=` preselect the Level filter; a deep link to one item (`?content=`) still opens on its shelf. **Not possible, said:** grouping «per Surah» — an `EducationalContent` row carries no Surah (§4.9; a Surah belongs to a class, an occurrence or an exam, R165 §2); adding one is an Owner decision on the model, not a page change.

**§4 — DEFECTS FOUND BY THE OWNER, FIXED.** (a) «تقويمي» still listed a completed Level in «المستوى»: R172 §14's predicate reached the calendar's rows but not its filter OPTIONS (`personalCalendarOptions`) — it does now, pinned. (b) «تسجيل الخروج» in the account menu and «مراجعة الطلب» in the notifications panel were near-white on white: R173's on-primary button rule for the dark bar reached into the light popovers — scoped to the bar's direct children; the sign-out ghost on the dark phone sheet made visible. (c) No notification bell on a phone — it now rides beside the burger. (d) «حفظي»'s sections, headings and completion badges sat flush against one another — the page has a rhythm now. (e) Table rows whose height followed the action buttons rather than the line: the actions `<td>` was `display: flex` and left the table's cell model; the cell is a cell again and the flex lives on an inner group (`DataTable` and the Hijri table). (f) Found in passing: a Trash row of R172 §1's `CategorySubject` read as a raw key — labelled.

**Revision 173 (Document Owner decision of 2026-09-24 — the interface redesigned for the women who will use it on their phones):** **BUILT AND VERIFIED; NO SCHEMA, ROUTE, ADAPTER, HOOK OR STATE CHANGE.** The Owner: *«completely redesign the UI/UX … modern, clean, professional, elegant … a lot of grown up women who are not good with technology will be using it mostly using their phones … intuitive, simple, easy to see and to scan … to make them want to come back to use it instead of going back to physical papers»*, under three strict rules — no API call, function, endpoint or state variable changed; the same data binding and flows; typography, spacing, colour hierarchy and responsive layout — and with a rulebook at the repository root. **The redesign is the stylesheet** (`frontend/src/styles/`): every page composes the shared components (§14.3), so the tokens and the component sheets carry it to every surface, and the 1 513 frontend tests, which pin the markup, pass unchanged.

**§1 — THE RULEBOOK.** [`design.mmd`](../design.mmd) at the repository root is the visual rulebook every screen answers to, below this document and the UX rules and above taste: identity, colour roles with measured contrast, the two faces and the scale, tap floors, motion, page anatomy, states, copy, the audit checklist and how a change is verified. The [design-system page](architecture/design-system.md) keeps the stylesheet's architecture and points to it.

**§2 — ONE DOMINANT COLOUR, ONE WARM ACT.** Deep zellij emerald is the frame (the header bar is now the brand's dark band on every screen; the current page is a lit pill in it) and every primary control; saffron — the association's orange at a value that carries text (6.6:1) or, as a fill, carries ink (8.3:1) — is the single warm act of a screen (the hero's «تسجيل الدخول», the sign-in on the bar, an unread count). Neutrals are mint-biased. **Unchanged by decision:** light only (`color-scheme: light`); the two brand values on the calendar's numerals with their accepted contrast (R-series record in `primitives.css`); the exam's violet (R58).

**§3 — TWO FACES, SELF-HOSTED, UNDER A BUDGET.** Readex Pro for everything read or pressed, El Messiri for what a page is called. §3.1's CSP (`default-src 'self'`) admits same-origin files, and §2.2's connection is honoured by SUBSETTING: the two Arabic files are 45 KB together — less than `logo.png` — the two Latin files 55 KB, each loaded only for its `unicode-range`, hashed into the immutable `/assets/` path, `font-display: swap`. The earlier «no web font» reasoning (a linked host is blocked; an inlined face is 200 KB–1 MB) stands and is why THIS shape was chosen; the rulebook fixes the ceiling at 120 KB and forbids any third-party host. Licences (SIL OFL) ship beside the files. **The body is 17 px** — one step above the web's default, for the readers named above.

**§4 — TOUCH AND SCAN.** Nothing pressable is under 44 px, fields and a phone's primary actions are 48 px; a field's label is body-sized and dark and read before the box; focus is a ring, never a colour change alone; a table is one surface with hairline rows and a pointed-at row tints; the dialog rises and reads as a bottom sheet on a phone; the mobile menu unfolds from the bar in the bar's own ground. Motion is CSS only — three durations, one settling curve, `prefers-reduced-motion` collapses all of it.

**§5 — FOUND AND FIXED IN PASSING:** on a phone, a wide table's start-side overflow reached the DOCUMENT (RTL: a scroller that is not its content's containing block does not clip it), so «حلقات المواد» and every other wide list could be panned ~190 px to the left onto nothing — it pre-dated the redesign; `.datatable__scroll` is now `position: relative` and the page-shooting harness reports the document width beside every shot. The sign-in page, which was `<main>` taking the page frame's slack, is a raised sheet that does not stretch to the phone's height.

**§6 — WHAT WAS DELIBERATELY NOT DONE.** No string of the Arabic catalogue was changed: the copy is the Owner's, governed by §2.1 and the terminology guard, and the audit found no placeholder text to replace («قيد الإعداد» names the sections the registry says are not built). No dark theme (the Owner's standing decision). No markup, adapter, hook, route or state change — the redesign was required to leave them exactly as they were, and did. The tables' column layout on a laptop (a wide list scrolls inside its own box) and the month grid's chips on a phone (the list view is the reading view) are the shapes the UX rules already decide.

**Revision 172 (Document Owner decisions of 2026-09-23 — a Subject taught to a whole Category, a series entered after the fact, a recording that outlives the screen, an exam deleted with its work, and three smaller items; PART TWO the same day: one occurrence deleted, the Staging fixtures withdrawn, two smaller items, and «الأستاذة» before every teacher's name; PART THREE the same day: a weekly class follows its start date, a completed Level leaves her calendar and library, and a circle is created in a branch):** **ALL FIFTEEN BUILT AND VERIFIED.** Two migrations (`20260926090000_r172_subject_taught_to_a_whole_category` — one join table; `20260926100000_r172_teaching_group_branch` — one nullable column, back-filled). Four routes added (`GET|PUT|DELETE /admin/categories/{id}/subjects[/{subjectId}]`, `DELETE /sessions/{id}`); `POST /uploads/initiate` accepts `category_id` in place of `level_id`; `DELETE /exams/{id}` gains `?acknowledge_evidence=true`. Everything else is additive.

**§1 — A SUBJECT TAUGHT TO A WHOLE CATEGORY** (the Owner: *«some subjects are taught to the category women, including all levels of it, and even any woman can join, such as الفقه and السيرة النبوية»*). `CategorySubject` is the second source beside `LevelSubject` (§4.4b): one row says every live Level of the Category teaches the Subject, present and future, read through the Level's `category_id` at the moment of the read and never copied down. **The curriculum policy answers from both** (`subjectsTaughtAt`, `levelsTeaching`, `assertSubjectTaughtAtLevel`), so every surface follows without a second rule: **(a) scheduling** — «الكل» on Level for a Category (R169 §7) reaches every Level of that Category; and the form's Subject may be chosen with no Level in play — R169 §7 promised this and the selector still disabled it with «اختاري المستوى أولًا», which is what the Owner met; **(b) attendance** — nothing new to build: the occurrence's audience is every enrolled beneficiary of those Levels, and who may mark (herself, the staff, the administration) is the class's `attendance_marking` (R123) as before; **(c) content** — an item may be filed for a whole Category with no Level chosen (`category_id` at initiation; the server files it under the Category's first Level with R167 §5's `whole_category`, the rule the recording ingest already applies), through a Subject the Category is taught; the Level control offers «{الفئة} — كل مستويات الفئة» wherever some Subject is taught whole; **(d)** the scope options list these Subjects under every Level of the Category and beside the Category; the public programme, the personal calendar's Subject filter and BR-11's «examined» Levels read the policy; a Subject's deletion tombstones its Category links (optional in older snapshots, so a Subject deleted before this revision stays restorable). Managed on «مواد المستوى», first table («مواد لكل مستويات الفئة»); Super Admin curriculum (R26, R43.3); removal never refused (what stands keeps its explicit Levels).

**§2 — A SERIES ENTERED AFTER THE FACT GETS ITS DATES** (the Owner: a public محاضرة for 14–18 September, created on the 22nd, «didn't show in the calendar»). Found: materialisation started at today, so a schedule whose dates lay wholly in the past produced NOTHING, silently — and `anchor_date` (R55: «starts the series») bounded only `none` and the biweekly parity, so a daily or weekly class said to begin next month began today. Both fixed in the one place a rule becomes dates (`expandSchedule`: the anchor is the lower bound of every pattern) and in the materialiser's third guarantee, restated: **the past is protected once it exists — a schedule that has never produced an occurrence starts at its own first date** (and «today» is Morocco's day). The nightly pass applies this to the Owner's schedule on Staging, which then shows its five occurrences.

**§3 — A LIBRARY RECORDING IS NAMED LIKE A CLASS RECORDING.** `suggested_recording_name` on `GET /library` is composed by `sessionRecordingBaseName` with what the screen has: «تسجيل صوتي» as the type, the Subject in view when there is one, no Surah, the person recording by her public display name (§20 rule 12), and the association's date and time — numbered against the shelf. It is never `null` for a signed-in caller any more (it was, with no Subject in view, and the Owner's phone then asked her to type one).

**§4 — THE RECORDING OUTLIVES THE SCREEN** (the Owner: *«turning off the screen should not stop the recording, also leaving the page of the recorder should not stop recording»*). The `MediaRecorder` no longer belongs to a component: one application-wide `RecordingSession` owns it; any screen's recorder is a view bound by its origin (`library`, `session:<id>`); a bar at the root shows the same controls on every page while a recording is active and no screen shows it, with «العودة» and, for a recording started from a class occurrence, the link to it on save. **Every ten seconds the captured chunk is written to the phone's own storage** (IndexedDB, per origin, never leaving the device; cleared on save or discard), so a reload, a crash or a closed tab leaves a recording the next visit offers back with its clock honest to the last chunk. **The screen is kept awake** (Wake Lock) while recording; a lock the person takes herself continues capture where the browser allows it (Android Chrome), and where it does not (iOS Safari) the track's own `mute` event raises the capture-gap notice — said in the recorder's standing text, which now promises what it can and warns of what it cannot. One recording at a time; a second screen meets a notice and the bar.

**§5 — THE ACCESS TOKEN IS RENEWED, NOT MERELY OBTAINED** (the Owner's failed 65-minute save: «تعذّر حفظ التسجيل»). Found: TD-12's one-hour access token was fetched once and never refreshed, so every tab older than an hour failed every call with 401 until reloaded — the save was that tab's first request past the hour, and the generic message hid it. Now `api()` renews on a 401 to a bearer request and retries once, and the session renews five minutes before expiry and when a hidden tab returns; a session that is genuinely over still ends. Platform-wide.

**§6 — AN EXAM WITH RECORDED WORK MAY BE DELETED, REVERSIBLY** (the Owner, on three test exams: *«if you think that it's ok to allow administration to delete exams that were taken by students and graded by teachers, then find a solution»*; this supersedes the 2026-09-03 refusal-only rule). The answer is R170 §8's shape for an exam: `DELETE /exams/{id}` is refused with the counts (`STUDENT_EVIDENCE_EXISTS`) until the administration acknowledges them (`?acknowledge_evidence=true`) — the screen asks a second time with «N ورقة وM نقطة» in view; the papers, answers, marks and attendance then stay attached to the tombstone, hidden from every reader, restored with the exam within the seven days, and **destroyed with it** when the Trash lets it go (`EXAM_HAS_RECORDED_EVIDENCE` is no longer a purge refusal; the audit carries the counts). The acknowledgement is the administration's: a مؤطِّرة still meets the explanation. Her three test exams were deleted that way on Staging (four rows — «الاختبار 1» existed twice).

**§7 — «الاختبارات» DELETES A PAPER.** The builder's library gains «حذف» per row — the same route, the same Trash, the same second question.

**§8 — «مؤطّرة هذه الحصة» IS GONE FROM «حصص الجدول»** (the Owner: it duplicated «من يؤطِّر هذه الحصة» in «تعديل الحصة», R166 §2). The row action and its dialog are removed; the one-off cover is unchanged in substance. Found in passing and fixed: the file only passed the unsaved-changes guard because that dialog was a `FormDialog` — «تعديل الحصة» and the cancellation dialog now carry the shared guard themselves.

**§9 — ONE OCCURRENCE MAY BE DELETED** (the Owner, 2026-09-23: «in حصص الجدول, allow deleting a session»). `DELETE /sessions/{id}?version=` — a cancellation says *this class did not happen*; a deletion says *this occurrence should not exist* (a stray one, a duplicate). Soft, with a Trash snapshot and an audit row; restorable for the seven days (a future occurrence restored into a slot given away since is `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT`, R171 §6's rule); destroyed with its attendance and recordings when the Trash lets it go (R170 §8's rule for a class's occurrences); **an exam sat in it keeps it** (`SESSION_HAS_EXAM`) — the sitting is deleted through its own door (§6). Administrative; the materialiser never re-creates a tombstoned occurrence. «حذف» beside «إلغاء» on «حصص الجدول».

**§10 — THE STAGING FIXTURES ARE WITHDRAWN** (the Owner, 2026-09-23: «after finishing the tests, delete all [تجريبي] data»). `ops:remove-fixtures` removes §15.2's `[تجريبي]` rows from a non-Production database through the platform's own deletion doors and the Trash purge — content with its objects, exams with their evidence, events, class schedules with their occurrences, fixture accounts through R111's purge (de-identified, as every deleted account is kept), then groups, rooms and branches. **A fixture row something of the Owner's still references is renamed, not deleted** — on Staging her own classes are held at «مقر تاركة» and «مقر أمرشيش» and their rooms; those keep her records and lose the tag (audited, `ops.fixture_untagged`). The Staging pipeline no longer runs `seed:fixtures` on an upgrade (deployment handbook); the seed drill proves the removal on a fresh database. §19.0's firewall stands: no real beneficiary data reaches Staging — what it holds from now on is the Owner's own UAT entries.

**§11 — TWO SMALLER ITEMS.** «حذف نهائي للحساب» closes its dialog once the account is gone (it stayed open over the notice). «تسجيل صوتي» / «إضافة عنصر» in «مكتبة المحتوى» ask for the Category first and offer «{الفئة} — كل مستويات الفئة» in the Level list ALWAYS (§1 offered it only once a Subject was assigned whole, and rule 2 of the dependent selectors then dropped the choice the moment it was made — the Owner could not file for a Category at all); a Category taught no Subject whole says so and names «مواد المستوى».

**§12 — «الأستاذة» BEFORE EVERY TEACHER'S NAME, DEFINED ONCE** (the Owner, 2026-09-23). Every composed title and recording name carries the word before the main teacher's public display name — «محاضرة — دورة علوم القرآن — الأستاذة فاطمة بوخبزى — 2026-09-22 15:00» — and a library recording carries no type word: «المادة — الأستاذة من سجّلت — التاريخ والوقت». **One place to change it:** `TEACHER_HONORIFIC` in `backend/src/lib/item-title.ts`, the composer every title flows through (class, occurrence, bare exam, the platform's own recording, the library's suggestion); the browser's live preview receives it from `/me/scope-options` (`teacher_honorific`) and keeps no copy. Titles composed at read time change at once; a recording's stored name carries the word from the moment it was made. **Not the catalogue's role word:** §2.1/R71's «مؤطِّرة» remains what the interface calls a member of staff, and the terminology guard still refuses «أستاذة» in the Arabic catalogue — the honorific is a title's word, the Owner's choice, defined in code and sent to the browser, never typed into `ar.ts`.

**§13 — A WEEKLY CLASS FOLLOWS ITS START DATE ON EDIT** (the Owner, 2026-09-23: «editing تاريخ البداية from 5 أكتوبر 2026 to 6 أكتوبر 2026 should reschedule the sessions to tuesdays instead of mondays, but it didn't»). Found in the browser's adapter, not the server: a plain «أسبوعيًا» class has no weekday control — its weekday IS its start date's — but on edit the adapter sent the row's STORED weekday whenever one was stored, so the server was told «Tuesday the 6th, on Mondays» and kept Mondays. The weekday is now derived from the start date on every save of the plain weekly pattern (`weekdaysForClass`, `frontend/src/adapters/scheduling.ts`); the patterns with their own weekday control (the R87 «أيام محددة» family) are unchanged. The materialiser then re-dates the future occurrences as R55/R172 §2 already say.

**§14 — A COMPLETED LEVEL LEAVES HER CALENDAR AND HER LIBRARY** (the Owner, 2026-09-23: «a student who finished a level … should stop seeing that level in تقويمي and in مكتبة المحتوى as those two pages should show what will help the student to study for the levels she is in progress of studying them»). One predicate — `inProgressEnrolmentWhere` in `policies/level-completion.ts` — says which of her enrolments are IN PROGRESS: live, at a Level she holds no completion mark for (R167 §3's `LevelCompletionMark`, the administration's verdict, R168 §3). «تقويمي» (`personalFilters`), «مكتبة المحتوى»'s private tier (`privateLevelIds`, for her and for her parent) and the student's own identity block (`GET /students/me`) read through it. **The enrolment is not touched:** it stays her record, R122's rule and the certificate's referent, and the administration's screens (§14.2, the roster, the Quran page, «سجل الطالبة») still show it; lifting the mark («إلغاء تسجيل الإتمام») restores the Level to both pages at once. Nothing is stored — the pages read the mark as it stands.

**§15 — A CIRCLE IS CREATED IN A BRANCH** (the Owner, 2026-09-23: «إضافة حلقة should include also branches, as a circle is created in a branch, each branch has its list of circles, same as for groups are created linked to a branch»). `teaching_group.branch_id` (nullable, `RESTRICT`), as `administrative_group.branch_id` is: «إضافة حلقة» asks for الفرع, «حلقات المواد» shows and filters by it, and `PATCH /admin/teaching-groups/{id}` accepts `branch_id` so a circle from before this revision can be placed. **This reverses R43.3's «a circle carries no branch» and keeps its authority split:** the branch says where the circle LIVES, not who owns it — a Level still spans branches (§4.4b), circle structure stays Super Admin's, membership stays Admin's scoped by the branch the STUDENT is enrolled at, and a class's branch is still the class's own (§4.4c). Existing circles were back-filled from their live classes where those all met at one branch and stay «—» otherwise until placed. **Registration (R168 §1):** a circle placed at another branch is not offered at this one even if a class of it meets here; a circle placed here or not yet placed is offered as before. The Owner's separate report — the women's registration form offering NO circles although three classes exist — is a different cause (the form offers only circles of the Subject that tracks memorisation, and hers are «أحكام التجويد») and is proposed to her in the hand-off with §1's semester question, not decided here.

**Revision 171 (independent codex review of the whole project at Revision 170 part five, 2026-09-22 — fourteen defects fixed, one release obligation met early, four reconciliations recorded, three gates named as still owed):** **FIXED, VERIFIED, NO SCHEMA CHANGE.** No new column, table or migration; every fix corrects a rule the schema already carries or a script's own order of operations. Two items are NOT fixes: §12 meets a Phase-2 obligation (§3.1a) ahead of the freeze because it needs no Production host, and §13 records what the dependency audit leaves and why.

**§1 — HIGH: erasure destroys the Level-completion mark too.** `destroyEducationalRecord` (R133/B7) collected every educational table of a person except `LevelCompletionMark` (added by Revision 167 §3 after the erasure list was written), so a de-identified account kept its certificate rows — a name-free row, but her record. Collected and deleted with the rest; pinned in the lifecycle test.

**§2 — HIGH: «today» is Morocco's day, not UTC's.** `calendarDay()` (`policies/effective-staffing.ts`) truncated to UTC midnight; between 00:00 and 01:00 Morocco time (summer) every «effective from today» staffing, the Trash's «past session» line and the teaching-candidate window were a day behind. It now truncates through `moroccoDateIso` (Revision 167 §1's clock); `trash.service` and `teaching-candidates.service` use it instead of their own `setUTCHours(0)`.

**§3 — HIGH: a recovery assembled from segments never overwrites the recorder's own file.** Revision 168 §2(d) wrote the assembly to the recording's `output_key` — the very key the recorder uploads its complete file to if it turns out to have been alive. The assembly is now written to `<key>.recovered.mp4`, the row's `output_key` repointed on adoption (and the assembly deleted if the row was taken by then); a recorder's own `completed` report arriving after a recovery and before the import TAKES THE ROW BACK (`recovered_from_segments` cleared, `output_key` restored) — the complete file always wins, as §2(c) promised.

**§4 — HIGH: three recording states the reconcile pass could not represent.** (a) a recording `starting` with no provider handle for longer than the grace window is now `failed` («start lost before the provider handle was recorded») rather than live forever; (b) a `stopping` row whose provider still says `recording` has its stop RETRIED on every pass (the first stop was swallowed); (c) the failed scan no longer stops at seven days — a row with an ingestion reason is re-examined however old, and a row with nothing to recover is marked «no segments to recover» once, so the scan stays bounded (`take: 500`).

**§5 — HIGH: the assembler cannot hang after ffmpeg exits.** The segment loop awaited `drain` on a closed stdin; it now races drain/close/exit and stops writing the moment the child is gone, and the exit is what decides success.

**§6 — HIGH: an occurrence's own edits obey §4.4.** `overrideSession` (room, date, times, staff) and `restoreSession` bypassed `findConflicts` — a class could be moved into a busy room, or restored into a slot given away while it was cancelled. Both now ask `assertOccurrenceFree` (a one-off candidate against materialised sessions, the series' own check) and answer `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT`. An end at or before the start, MERGED with what the row already holds, is `VALIDATION_FAILED · END_NOT_AFTER_START`, not a 500.

**§7 — MEDIUM: regenerating an occurrence returns EVERY override.** `regenerateOne` cleared the flag and the room/times but kept an overridden delivery, media mode, visibility, Subject and Surahs. It now resets all of them to the class's.

**§8 — MEDIUM: Surah inheritance is one rule in every reader and writer.** A choice equal to the class's own Surahs is stored as inheritance (`[]`), so a later edit of the class reaches that date; and the class's Surahs are inherited ONLY while the Subject taught that day works by Surah — an occurrence retaught as فقه shows none (calendar, composed title). Revision 165 §5's «none means the class's» is unchanged in meaning.

**§9 — MEDIUM: three check-then-write rows are now serialised or reconciled.** (a) The certificate's issue/withdraw and the mark's removal lock the mark row (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`; TD-15.2), so two issues draw ONE number and an unmark cannot delete the row from under an issue; a double-tapped mark answers like a repeat. (b) Re-marking a withdrawn attendance removes its Trash tombstone in the same transaction (the LevelSubject rule), so the Trash never advertises a live mark as removed. (c) A content item's Subject change, or its home-Level change, is checked against the home Level AND every additional Level it keeps (`SUBJECT_NOT_IN_LEVEL`), not only the Levels the request names.

**§10 — MEDIUM: the recovery point asks whether a class is being recorded.** `create-recovery-point.sh` stopped the API without asking; Revision 167 §5 made a deployment ask. It now runs `ops:active-recordings` inside the writer before anything stops — exit 3 postpones (the daily timer retries tomorrow), an unanswered check is a refusal too, and only a fixture drill may skip it. Pinned by the CI guard and the drill.

**§11 — MEDIUM: `EGRESS_LIMIT_REACHED` is a failure.** The provider status was untranslated, so a recorder stopped at its limit reported an unknown state instead of `failed`.

**§12 — §3.1a Phase 2, met early: every third-party image is pinned by digest.** `postgres`, `livekit-server`, `egress`, `redis`, `certbot` in `docker-compose.yml` and `node`/`nginx` in both Dockerfiles carry the index digest their tags resolved to on Staging (SeaweedFS and ffmpeg already did); `check-compose-operations.sh` refuses an unpinned image. Same versions — a re-pushed tag changes nothing until an Owner-approved upgrade task.

**§13 — dependency audit, recorded.** Patch-level fixes (`qs`, `fast-uri`, `brace-expansion`, `nanoid`, `postcss`, `vitest`) in their own commit (§3.1a Phase 1). What remains is one chain under the `prisma` CLI (`@prisma/dev` → `find-my-way`/`valibot`/`mysql2`; `@prisma/config` → `deepmerge-ts`), fixed only by a major upgrade §3.1a forbids without a task: none of it runs here (no MySQL, no local dev server; the CLI's only Production act is `migrate deploy`). Accepted with that reason, to be re-audited in the upgrade task.

**§14 — reconciliations.** (a) The readiness page no longer says TD-3 has no operational-alert read — Revision 169 §11 shipped it; backup freshness and certificate expiry are still host-only and are said to be. (b) No remote backup target exists or is claimed anywhere (B8). (c) The audit's free-text question is closed by Revision 170 §18. (d) TD-3's eighth strict-gate entry, `GET /jobs/{id}`, is recorded beside the seven: no endpoint issues a `job_id`, so it is unreachable as written — the Owner's call, like the `/grades/{id}` shapes.

**§15 — still owed, named.** A generated permission-matrix test, J1–J8 as automation, and the ≥80 % coverage threshold (§19.2) are absent; each is bounded and needs no Production host. Recorded on the readiness page.

**Revision 170 (Document Owner decisions of 2026-09-21, third batch — PART ONE BUILT: «صفاتي وطلباتي» never disappears, and the registration form is redesigned around one closed role chooser; PART TWO BUILT: a Category says who holds the login and its age range, every beneficiary carries a reference code, a refused applicant may be told the reason, the 90-day quarantine sweep, a deleted class leaves the Trash with its occurrences, a مؤطِّرة lists every activity her calendar shows, and two decisions found ALREADY built; PART THREE BUILT: the consent gate becomes a warning; PART FOUR BUILT: §8 completed at the Owner's word (attendance and recordings go with the class), and the landing page redesigned; PART FIVE BUILT: what the audit keeps (§18), the circle picker (§19), and three Staging defects; THE REST IS RECORDED HERE AS DECIDED AND IS BUILT PART BY PART, each part saying so when it lands):** the Owner answered the Revision 169 report. Part one needs no migration (one response gains a field: `GET /profile/role-requests` → `held[]`). Part two adds one migration (`20260925090000_r170_category_own_login_and_reference_code_for_every_beneficiary`: three nullable columns and a CHECK on `category`, one column on `role_request`, a trigger and a CHECK on `user`); part three one more (`20260925100000_r170_consent_gate_becomes_a_warning`: one column); every wire change is additive except that `CONSENT_FORCED_PRIVATE` is no longer a refusal.

**§1 — «صفاتي وطلباتي» IS ALWAYS ON «حسابي»** (the Owner: *«I don't find this»*). She could not, and the cause was the design, not her: the section rendered only when there was a request to list or a role to ask for, and her own account — provisioned, never «registered», holding every role — has neither; a failed read removed it for anybody. It now always renders: **the roles she holds** (`held[]`, from live role rows — the one addition to the read), her requests under their own heading, «طلب صفة إضافية» whenever something is askable, and otherwise the sentence that says nothing can be asked for now and that the button appears when something can. A failed read is shown as a failure, in the one appearance every failure has.

**§2 — THE REGISTRATION FORM IS REDESIGNED, AND «ماذا تريدين؟» IS ONE CLOSED CONTROL WITH «أسجّل نفسي كمستفيدة» CHOSEN** (the Owner: *«more user friendly, easy to use, beautiful and elegant … a closed drop down, by default selecting أسجّل نفسي كمستفيدة, clicking on it expands all the choices, and multiple choices can be ticked as it's done right now»*). **This reverses one sentence of Revision 168 §1 («Nothing is preselected on the form»), by her decision**; everything else R168 §1 says stands — four roles, any combination, one applicant, each role decided on its own. The chooser is the platform's own multi-select: closed until opened, real checkboxes inside, its closed face NAMING what is chosen (and wrapping rather than cutting her choice off). The default is a default, never a lock: unticking it erases what its section held, and a form with nothing chosen is still refused (`roles`). The page itself: three steps said before she starts (the last — «تراجع الإدارة طلبك» — is not hers); every section a card with a numbered heading, the numbers following the sections actually shown; two columns where there is room and one on a phone; **what she must give before what she may skip** — the optional French pair and the كنية close each person's section as one block, the pair's rule said once instead of under both boxes. No field is added, removed or renamed; the request is unchanged.

**§4 — EDITING AN ALREADY-SCHEDULED REMOTE SITTING'S ARRANGEMENTS** (the Owner: *«should be built»*) — **found already built, not built twice.** Revision 145 §1 (2026-09-15) gave it `PATCH /exams/{id}/schedule` and الجدولة's «تعديل» for an online sitting — date, time, target, availability policy, catalogue type, visibility, Surah — and Revision 146 §2 its staff; ten HTTP tests hold it. The Owner asked again because one ledger box still said «deferred»; that box is closed with this evidence.

**§5 — A مؤطِّرة'S قائمة LISTS EVERY ACTIVITY HER CALENDAR SHOWS** (the Owner: *yes*). `GET /events` kept a narrower rule of its own for a teacher — what she staffs, or what is addressed to her Administrative Groups and to nothing else — so an activity for her Level, her branch or her Category, which her calendar showed and her students were expected at, was absent from «جدولي». The calendar's rule (§4.4c, R71.2, R109, R169 §6 — the named dimensions INTERSECT her teaching scope; a dimension left empty is «الكل»; she staffs it; or she is responsible for a hidden one) is now ONE policy, `teacherEventVisibility`, and both reads use it. Editing is unchanged: she edits her own group's activities only.

**§6 — A CATEGORY SAYS WHO HOLDS THE LOGIN, AND ITS AGE RANGE** (the Owner: *«yes, add a tick-box on each Category … the age range for each category»*; closes the gap Revision 64 (7) recorded). `Category.holds_own_login` — `true`: its beneficiaries sign in themselves, so it is offered to a woman registering HERSELF and refused on a child application (`CATEGORY_HOLDS_OWN_LOGIN`); `false`: a guardian registers them, the reverse (`CATEGORY_IS_GUARDIAN_MANAGED`); **`null` is «not stated» and restricts nothing**, so a Category nobody has answered for behaves exactly as before. Existing rows are answered from `self_attendance_allowed` (R123), the one structural fact already held, and the Owner may change either on «الفئات». The three registration forms stop OFFERING what would be refused and the server refuses regardless. **It binds the request, never the placement** (R39, R66.5), and **it is not an age gate** (R64.7): `min_age` / `max_age` are informational, shown on «الفئات» and beside the Category's name on the forms («اليافعات (من 13 إلى 17 سنة)»), 0–120 whole years, never inverted (`AGE_RANGE_INVERTED`), and they refuse nothing.

**§7 — EVERY BENEFICIARY CARRIES A SPOKEN REFERENCE CODE, ADULTS INCLUDED** (the Owner: *«yes, every beneficiary get one»*). R62.5 gave one to children only, minted by the one service that admits a child; a person becomes a beneficiary on several write paths, so the guarantee lives on the row: trigger `user_beneficiary_reference_code_fill` mints `BA-` + five characters of the same alphabet (no 0/O, no 1/I/L; random, never sequential) whenever a live row becomes a beneficiary without one, and CHECK `user_beneficiary_reference_code_check` holds the rule; a de-identified row is exempt (R133 clears its code on purpose), and a code is never reissued. Back-filled by the migration. It is SHOWN on «حسابي», on «المستخدمون» and in the staff people-picker (`reference_code` on `GET /admin/directory` — non-personal by design, and exactly what a picker is for), and «المستخدمون»'s search finds a person by it as it is SAID («7k4m2», «ba7k4m2», «BA-7K4M2»), matched whole. **It identifies; it never authorises** (R62.5) — unchanged.

**§8 — A DELETED CLASS LEAVES THE TRASH AFTER THE SAME SEVEN DAYS AS EVERYTHING ELSE, WITH ITS OCCURRENCES** (the Owner: *«a deleted class keep its past sessions visible in the Trash for the same period … to keep a consistency of that rule»*). **This supersedes Revision 118 (1)** («one Session makes the schedule permanent»). Deleting a class now takes its PAST occurrences to the Trash with it (they stayed live under a tombstoned schedule: on no calendar, in no Trash), and a restore gives them back with the future ones. After the window the class is destroyed WITH its occurrences — each with its owned rows — **and, at the Owner's word (2026-09-22, answering the one open point of this section), with their attendance and their recordings**: attendance rows go with the occurrence; a recording the occurrence produced becomes an ordinary deleted library item (Trash entry, exact-key quarantine obligation, destroyed by `trash.retention-purge` after its own seven days) rather than an orphan. **What still keeps a class is an occurrence an EXAM was sat in**: an exam's answers, grades and attendance are the exam's evidence (Revision 136, `EXAM_HAS_RECORDED_EVIDENCE`) and never go with a class — such a class stays in the Trash as retained (R118 (2)), coded `SESSIONS_HAVE_EXAMS`, and the screen says so. A live occurrence is never destroyed from under the calendar. *This narrows Revision 133 (4)'s «shared institutional data survives» for one case: a class the administration deleted and did not restore within seven days.*

**§9 — A CLOSED BRANCH, ROOM OR SUBJECT THAT OLD RECORDS STILL POINT TO IS KEPT FOR EVER, AND MAY GO ONCE NOTHING POINTS TO IT** (the Owner: *«I would say yes»*) — **confirmed as built; nothing changed.** This is Revision 118 (2): such a row is presented as RETAINED, neither restorable nor purgeable while a schedule, an enrolment, a group or an exam names it, and the nightly `trash.retention-purge` destroys it on the first night nothing does (a blocked purge is counted, never forced).

**§10 — QUARANTINED FILES ARE DESTROYED AUTOMATICALLY AFTER NINETY DAYS** (the Owner: *«destroy automatically»*; closes R59.4's open decision). A REPLACED file's old object waited in `quarantine/` for ever: a deleted item's object goes with its Trash entry, but a replacement has no Trash entry and nothing named a day. `content.quarantine-sweep` (TD-7; daily 03:30 Africa/Casablanca, one bounded page per job, the next enqueued in a transaction — the twin of `upload.gc`, one profile of the same loop) deletes objects under `quarantine/` in both buckets strictly older than ninety days **that are owed to nobody**: an object of a content that has a Trash entry (its own purge or restore owns it) or an unfinished storage obligation is retained, and a key that is not `quarantine/<uuid>/…` is never touched. Counts are logged, never a key (TD-14). BR-15's seven-day Trash window (R133) is untouched: this is the object store's ninety days, the number the Owner gave.

**§11 — A REFUSED APPLICANT MAY BE TOLD THE REAL REASON** (the Owner: *«yes, optionally, to be decided by the super admin/admin»*). Both decline paths — `POST /admin/approvals/{id}/reject` and `…/roles/{kind}/decline` — accept `share_reason: true`, **unticked each time and never the default**; what was shared is written to its own column (`role_request.shared_decline_reason`) and is what `GET /profile/role-requests` shows her under the declined request (`shared_reason`, word for word). The operator-facing `decline_reason` never leaves the office (§5.6 stands); re-asking clears both; the audit row records `reason_shared`. The decline notification is unchanged — the sentence is read on «حسابي».

**§12 — RECORDED, NOTHING TO BUILD** (the Owner: *no* and *NO*): no guardianship document is checked or recorded, and health, family situation and home address are NOT collected for minors. R62.1's collected set stands as the whole of it.

**§14 — KEPT AS THEY ARE** (the Owner: *keep*): the circles offered come from scheduled classes (R168 §1); a returning مستفيدة is asked nothing more; teaching is the one role مؤطِّرة; the مؤطِّرة's Quran list stays names-only for now.

**§3 — THE CONSENT GATE BECOMES A WARNING** (the Owner: *«When a class contains a beneficiary whose guardian refused media release, just a warning should be shown for the teacher, admin/super admin, but nothing should be forced, keep the by default public, and it's up to the teacher/admin/super admin to read the warning and switch manually to private»*). **BUILT (part three). This reverses BR-2 and BR-3 as written, §4.9's «hard constraint», §4.1a's «force-corrects», the visibility state machine's forced transition, TD-2's «override consent gate» row, `CONSENT_GATE_LOCKED` and §20 rule 7's first clause — each is annotated in place below and this paragraph is the rule now.** The re-evaluation engine is UNCHANGED in what it computes and when (every §4.1a trigger, the same resolved audience, the same union over the Sessions a recording is linked to, the same «absence is no consent», the same zero-audience edge case); what it WRITES changed: `media_consent_missing` on the recording — *a student in this recording's audience has no media release* — maintained in BOTH directions (a later grant clears it; a warning describes the present, unlike the one-way safeguard it replaces), audited as `content.consent_warning`. **Nothing is forced and nothing is refused**: a recording's visibility is whatever it was set to (the Category default, public where the Owner set it so), `PATCH /content/{id}` publishes a warned item, the library and the Nginx public-object gate read `visibility` alone, and `consent_forced_private` is retired (never written; every `true` moved onto the warning; pending consent migrations completed with the code `withdrawn_r170`). **The warning is shown to staff and to nobody else** — a fact about a child: on «مكتبة المحتوى» beside the visibility control («تنبيه: … يُستحسن جعله «خاصًّا»») and as a badge on the tier; and on the class occurrence dialog BEFORE anything is recorded («تنبيه قبل التسجيل …»), from `audience_media_consent_missing` on the session read, which is `null` for every reader who is not staff. **Deliberately unchanged:** BR-1 (consent is recorded at registration and by staff; absence is refusal); `جاري التسجيل` (R99, transparency); who may record and who may edit an item; the Category default visibility (§4.9); every audit row the engine wrote before. One migration (`20260925100000_r170_consent_gate_becomes_a_warning`).

**§17 — THE LANDING PAGE IS REDESIGNED** (the Owner, 2026-09-22: *«beautiful, elegant, user friendly, easy to use»*). What changed is presentation and one addition; no fact, figure or promise: the hero's tiled motif is replaced by a calm gradient with one soft glow (CSS only, §2.2), the title balanced, and — for a VISITOR only (R138 item 9 stands: a signed-in reader gets no hero CTA block) — a second way in beside «تسجيل الدخول»: «اكتشفي برامجنا», an anchor to the programmes on the same page. **Three entry tiles** below the hero say what can be read without an account — البرامج التعليمية · الجدول الزمني · المحتوى التعليمي — each a link to a public route (§14.1). Programme cards gain a heading band (Category, its words, its Level count) and numbered Level rows; branch cards say each line with an icon; partners are a grid of equal cards. Data-driven exactly as before: `GET /programs`, `GET /branches`, `GET /partners`.

**§18 — WHAT THE AUDIT KEEPS** (the Owner, 2026-09-22, answering three questions): **(a) the login audit keeps the user id only** — `auth.login`/`auth.login_denied` carry `provider`, `account_status`/`reason` and the actor id; **the identity e-mail is not written** (TD-8's grid row is reconciled below — the code had already stopped writing it under TD-14, and the grid still said otherwise); **(b) storage coordinates reach the audit as a content id and a non-reversible digest** (`storageCoordinateId`, SHA-256 of bucket + key — already the practice in every content audit row; recorded as the rule); **(c) fixed codes in the audit, the sentence only on the record that owns it** — a decline/rejection/revocation audit row carries `reason_recorded: true` (and `reason_shared`, R170 §11) and a fixed code where one exists (`identity_review`), never the approver's or reviewer's sentence, which lives on `role_request.decline_reason` or `family_link.decision_reason` and goes with that record (R133). `familylink.approve|reject|revoke`, `familylink.identity_review`, `user.reject`, `rolerequest.decline` follow it; the exam question's `justification` is a rule enum and was never free text.

**§19 — THE CIRCLE-MEMBERSHIP PICKER OFFERS BENEFICIARIES BY DEFAULT** (the Owner, 2026-09-22): «إضافة مستفيدة إلى المجموعة» lists accounts with `is_beneficiary` (R79.7) and a tick-box «إظهار الجميع» widens it to every active account for the rare case. «التسجيلات» already united the Student role with that fact.

**§20 — RECORDED, NOT BUILT (the Owner, 2026-09-22): no automatic grading components before launch.** Per-exam marks stay the whole of grading; the weight-template engine remains where Revision 12 left it (§10.1) — revisited with a term of real marks.

**DECIDED BY THE OWNER IN THE SAME MESSAGE — RECORDED NOW, BUILT IN THE PARTS THAT FOLLOW (nothing below is implied built until its part says so):** **§13 —** the CNDP filing material and the Arabic privacy-notice text are drafted for her review. **§15 —** the performance measurement is run. **§16 —** the §18 acceptance checklists and end-to-end journeys start on Staging, and whatever Production needs that requires neither its server nor a payment is prepared. **Answered 2026-09-22 (§18–§20 above); nothing of this batch is open but §15 and §16.**

**Revision 169 (Document Owner decisions of 2026-09-21, second batch — PART ONE: an existing account asks for a further role, a new role needs no new sign-in, room capacity is published, a cancelled class is not «where a content was used», and five questions answered with no change to build; PART TWO: an activity's audience dimensions combine in every read, and a class may be addressed to «الكل»; PART THREE: the Trash restores a Subject circle, a class schedule and a Level; PART FOUR: every beneficiary carries a date of birth; PART FIVE: a recording or library item may belong to several Levels; PART SIX: «حالة النظام», the operational alert read):** the Owner answered the open-decision list of the Revision 168 report. **What follows is part one; the parts still to come are named at its end so nothing is implied built that is not.** Part one needs no migration (two routes and one DTO field are added; one response gains a field); part four adds one.

**§1 — AN ACCOUNT THAT ALREADY EXISTS MAY ASK FOR A FURTHER ROLE — AND AGAIN FOR ONE THAT WAS DECLINED** (the Owner: *yes* to both; this closes what Revision 168 §1 recorded as «not built, and said»). «حسابي» gains **«صفاتي وطلباتي»** — every role she asked for with its own state in words — and **«طلب صفة إضافية»** (`/profile/request-role`), which is the registration form's role sections themselves (one implementation, two callers), one role at a time, from a person the platform already knows: no name, no phone, no Google step. `GET /profile/role-requests` answers her requests and `askable` — never a role she holds, never one already waiting, and never `guardian`, because registering a child (`POST /child-applications`) IS that request. `POST /profile/role-requests` takes `student { branch_id, category_id, first_time, circle_preferences? }` with her consent to the wording in force and — ONLY where her record has none — a date of birth (R130: completion, never correction; `BIRTH_DATE_REQUIRED` / `BIRTH_DATE_ALREADY_RECORDED`), or `teaching { framing }`, or `administration { branch_id | null }` — a preference for where she would serve and never a role. **Asking still grants nothing**: it opens one `RoleRequest`, or **RE-OPENS the existing row** (`pending`, its decision cleared — one line of history per person and role, the two decisions kept by the audit log as `rolerequest.decline` and `rolerequest.reopen`), and tells the approvers by the same notice a registration raises. Refused: a role she holds (`409 ROLE_ALREADY_HELD`), a request already waiting (`409 ALREADY_PENDING`), an account that is not `active` (`403`). **The decision is Revision 168's, unchanged** — `POST /admin/approvals/{id}/roles/{kind}/approve|decline`, an administration request a Super Admin's alone — and declining a FURTHER role never touches the account she already has. The approval queue item gains `account_active`: such an item has no whole-account decision at all (there is no pending account to approve or reject), so the screen offers the per-role review only. **A declined `guardian` request is re-opened by registering a child**, so the new application is not dead on arrival (`GUARDIAN_REQUEST_DECLINED`); the decision stays the approver's. **The decline reason never reaches her** — it is the administration's note (§5.6); she is told THAT a request was declined.

**§2 — A NEWLY GRANTED ROLE NEEDS NO NEW SIGN-IN** (the Owner: *«either allow it to show without re-login, or force logout»*). It already shows, and the older note (*«appears only after re-login»*) was wrong by the time she read it: an access token is never trusted for longer than a page — every page load refreshes, and `POST /auth/refresh` reads the LIVE role assignments — so a granted role is hers at her next page, and she is told by the existing `role_assignments_changed` notice. Nothing is force-logged-out. What this revision adds is the PROOF (an HTTP test: a role granted mid-session is in the very next refresh of the same session, and in `GET /me`) and the statement, on «المستخدمون», of when a saved role takes effect.

**§3 — A ROOM'S CAPACITY IS PUBLISHED AND EDITABLE** (the Owner: *yes*). `RoomDto` carries `capacity` (`number | null` — *not stated*), `POST /admin/branches/{id}/rooms` and `PATCH /admin/rooms/{id}` accept it (a positive whole number or `null`; the column and its CHECK are Revision 43's), the rooms dialog asks for it, and the scheduling form's «سعة القاعة {n}» hint — written long ago and never once rendered, because the wire carried no number — finally appears beside the chosen room. **BR-23 and §20 rule 22 are untouched: capacity informs and refuses nothing.**

**§4 — A CANCELLED CLASS IS NOT NAMED AS WHERE A CONTENT WAS USED.** `GET /library/{id}/sessions` listed a cancelled occurrence; its link opened the calendar's day read, which excludes cancelled occurrences (R83.1), so the reader landed on «غير متاح». The backlink now lists what is ON, like every ordinary projection. The occurrence's record, retention and visibility are untouched, and `include_cancelled=true` still finds it on the calendar.

**§5 — ANSWERED, WITH NOTHING TO BUILD.** **(a) An Admin may see branch names in selectors** — R60's boundary is a visibility boundary by the Owner's decision, and stays one. **(b) «The server obeys the active role»** — it has since Revision 60: the claim is narrowed when the token is minted, the freshness check re-narrows from live rows (`ACTIVE_ROLE_REVOKED`), and an HTTP test proves a Super Admin acting as مؤطِّرة is refused `/admin/settings`. **(c) Categories and Levels carry descriptions** — they have since 2026-08-27 (column, forms, the public programme page, and the Owner's own texts in the production seed). **(d) One notification per decision; a declined role may be asked for again; the teaching choice grants the one role مؤطِّرة** (*assistant* stays a position on a class, R91) — all as built.

**§6 — AN ACTIVITY'S AUDIENCE DIMENSIONS COMBINE, IN EVERY READ** (part two; the Owner: *yes — activities combine their filters the way classes do*; this answers Revision 163 §5(i)). *Branch B1* with *Level Y* means the people in BOTH at once; two branches mean either; **a dimension left alone is «الكل» and narrows nothing**. Who an activity notifies, who is expected at it, and whose personal calendar it is on have read that way since Revision 140 — so this changes WHO IS NOTIFIED for no existing activity. What still UNIONed is corrected: **(a)** a مؤطِّرة's view of a PRIVATE activity — each dimension the activity names must reach her teaching scope (a Level she teaches at another branch no longer shows her an activity for *B1 ∧ Y*, which concerns nobody she teaches); a global activity falls out of the same rule; the activities she STAFFS are hers regardless, unchanged (R71.2), and the hidden tier stays ownership-only (R109). **(b)** the calendar's `level_id`, `category_id` and `administrative_group_id` filters — «the calendar of this Level» is every activity that CONCERNS it (its Category's, its branch's, a global one) and never another Category's; only `branch_id` read that way before. **(c)** the activity form — each audience filter reads «الكل» when untouched (it said «لم تُختَر جهة بعد»), and once anything is chosen it says how the choices combine. An activity still has no حلقة dimension: there is no such join (§7), and none is invented.

**§7 — A CLASS MAY BE ADDRESSED TO «الكل» ON LEVEL, GROUP AND CIRCLE: EVERY LEVEL THAT TEACHES ITS SUBJECT** (part two; the Owner: *yes*; this overturns the rule Revision 163 §5 kept — `MULTI_DIMENSION_NEEDS_A_LEVEL` is no longer raised for a class with a Subject). A class delivers a curriculum Subject, so «everybody» can only mean everybody who is TAUGHT it: the live Levels whose `LevelSubject` lists the Subject, narrowed to the Categories she chose if she chose any (branches narrow enrolments, as always). **It is resolved when the class is SAVED and stored as ordinary Level rows** — so the audience, her personal calendar, notifications, the Surahs on offer and a recording's Level all go on reading explicit Levels and learn no second rule, the *Subject taught at every Level it reaches* check (R43/R155) holds by construction, and on «تعديل» she SEES which Levels the class reaches. The stated cost: a Level that starts teaching the Subject later is added to the class by hand. A Subject NO Level teaches has nobody to reach and is refused in words — `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`, `NO_LEVEL_TEACHES_SUBJECT`. The form offers, with no Level in play, the Subjects SOME Level teaches (never the whole catalogue), and says beside the filters what «الكل» on all three means. One occurrence's audience override (`PUT /sessions/{id}/audience`) is unchanged: there «everybody» has no Subject-wide meaning to fall back on.

**§8 — THE TRASH RESTORES A SUBJECT CIRCLE, A CLASS SCHEDULE AND A LEVEL** (part three; the Owner: *build this*; §4.10/TD-5's restorable set widens by exactly the three types whose reinstatement is now written and tested). **A حلقة** returns with the seats its deletion released — except a student's who has since been seated in another circle of that Subject and Level, or who is no longer enrolled there; those are counted and said, never forced. **A class schedule** returns with the occurrences its deletion removed that are still ahead (by the ids its tombstone names — never ones materialisation would invent, never ones protection had spared); occurrences whose date has passed stay deleted; and the restore is **refused whole** (`SCHEDULE_CONFLICT`) when its room or a member of its staff has been booked since, by the scheduling form's own conflict check. **A Level** returns with its curriculum links, its «مقرر الحفظ» and its Administrative Groups, and the activities that were addressed to it or to its groups are re-addressed — for activities still live. Those activity links are hard-deleted and had never been recorded, so from this revision a Level's tombstone names them; a Level deleted EARLIER restores without them and the screen says its activities' audiences must be checked by hand. Each is refused while something it hangs from is itself in the Trash (`PARENT_DELETED`). `POST /admin/trash/{id}/restore` answers what came back — `seats_restored/not_restored`, `sessions_restored/not_restored`, `event_links_restored/unknown` — the audit row carries the same counts, and the screen says them, because «تمت الاستعادة» alone would hide a seat that could not return. A deleted schedule with held Sessions therefore appears in the actionable Trash view again (Decision B of 2026-09-02 kept it out because NEITHER button could work; one now does) and remains un-purgeable.

**§9 — EVERY BENEFICIARY CARRIES A DATE OF BIRTH** (part four; the Owner, verbatim: *«fill records without birth dates with any dates, just to be able to make birth date mandatory»* — this closes R130's contract phase, which had waited for every legacy record to be completed by hand). **A live beneficiary row with no date of birth can no longer exist** (`user_beneficiary_birth_date_check`). The column stays nullable, because a guardian-only adult and a staff request are never asked for one (R49, R130), and a de-identified row keeps its erasure. **The filled-in date is MARKED, never invented as a fact:** it is one fixed day (1900-01-01) stored with `birth_date_is_placeholder = true`, applied by the DATABASE (a trigger — so an approval, a per-role decision, a child application from before R130, a restore from the Trash or any future path fills it identically, and none can forget). **It is a mark, never an age:** every rule that reads a date of birth reads «not recorded» for it — read naively it would make a child *eligible at eighteen* for a self-managed account (R132) — and no screen is ever sent it as a date: the API answers `birth_date: null`, so «المستخدمون» and «حسابي» ask for the real one exactly as they did. **Recording the real date is COMPLETION (R130):** it replaces the placeholder once and clears the mark; *correcting* a recorded date remains refused (`BIRTH_DATE_ALREADY_RECORDED`). New registrations and child applications were already required to bring one. One migration: `20260924090000_r169_beneficiary_birth_date_required` (it filled 3 live records on Localhost and 9 on Staging).

**§10 — A RECORDING, OR ANY LIBRARY ITEM, MAY BELONG TO SEVERAL LEVELS** (part five; the Owner: *«if this can be changed to make any recording belonging to many levels do it»*; this lifts the limit Revision 166 declared — a private recording of a class over two Levels was listed for the first Level's beneficiaries only). `EducationalContent.level_id` stays the item's **home Level** — what its Subject is checked against and what §4.9's library is grouped by — and a new join, **`EducationalContentLevel`**, names its OTHER Levels. Purely additive: an item with none behaves exactly as before. **(a) Recordings.** The ingest files a class's recording under the first addressed Level, in the Levels' own order, and names the rest as additional Levels — unless the class covered every Level of one Category, where «كل مستويات الفئة» (R167 §5) already says so and naming them again would be a second copy of one fact. **(b) Who sees it.** §4.9's private tier admits a beneficiary enrolled at the home Level **or at any additional one**; the public and hidden tiers, the BR-2 consent gate (which is derived from the CLASS's audience, never from the item's Level) and staff visibility are unchanged — it widens WHICH Levels, never who. `GET /library?level_id=` and `?category_id=` read the additional Levels too, so the item is on each of its Levels' shelves, and every item carries `additional_levels[]`. **(c) Editing.** `PATCH /content/{id}` accepts `additional_level_ids`, which REPLACES the set: each must be a live Level that teaches the item's Subject (the rule its home Level already meets), and none may be the home Level (`LEVEL_IS_HOME`; a trigger refuses it too). Moving the home onto a Level that was an additional one stops naming it twice. The edit dialog asks «مستويات أخرى ينتمي إليها», and not alongside «كل مستويات الفئة». **(d)** A Level is not deleted while an item is filed under it OR also belongs to it. One migration: `20260924100000_r169_content_additional_levels`.

**§11 — «حالة النظام»: THE ADMINISTRATOR IS TOLD WHAT IS FAILING QUIETLY** (part six; the Owner: *yes*; TD-14/TD-16 had required an Admin-visible alert surface and nothing provided one). `GET /admin/operations/status`, **Super Admin only** (the counts span every branch), answers — in COUNTS — background jobs that failed for good and jobs late by more than ten minutes (in total and for the ten worst queues, by the platform's own queue names), and storage retirements that are pending, failed, late or of unknown copy outcome. **The definitions are the host monitor's own, verbatim**, so the screen and the monitor cannot disagree; **no payload, error text or key is ever sent** (TD-14). The screen (`/admin/operations`, the last node of الإدارة — appended to Revision 105's sequence, the Owner's to move) says what each number MEANS and what to do. **It is a read, never a notification**: nobody's inbox carries platform state (R77–R93 stand). **What it cannot see, it says:** backup freshness and the certificate's expiry live on the HOST, which the API container deliberately cannot read — `host_checks: "not_visible_from_here"`, stated in words, because a page of zeros must never read as «the backup is fine». Showing those two needs the host to publish its status where the API may read it; that is an infrastructure decision and is not taken here, and no certificate-expiry check exists anywhere yet.

**NOT built under this revision, and said:** the backup and certificate halves of §11 (above); and the automatic grading components the Owner asked for, which are the weight-template engine Revision 12 postponed to after launch (§10.1) — confirmed with her before any of it is started. **Owner questions still open, in one place:** [`TASKS.md`](TASKS.md) — Revision 169.

**Revision 168 (Document Owner decisions — a recorder that dies mid-class loses nothing recorded, a Level is completed when the administration records it, scheduling-type names are hers to change, the certificate's seal, and the registration form's four roles, ratified 2026-09-21):** **ALL FIVE BUILT AND VERIFIED — §2–§5 in this revision's first part, §1 in its second.** Two migrations (`20260923090000_r168_recording_recovered_from_segments` — one column; `20260923100000_r168_role_requests` — two tables, with every registration still pending back-filled). Every contract change is additive; one recording contract changes VALUE (§2: a صوت فقط recording is `audio/mp4`, no longer `audio/ogg`).

**§1 — ONE REGISTRATION FORM, FOUR ROLES, EACH DECIDED SEPARATELY** ([`SRS-PROPOSAL-R168.md`](SRS-PROPOSAL-R168.md), now ratified). The Owner's answer to its first and gating question: **yes — administrative staff may ask through the public form.** R49's *"administrator accounts arrive through staff pre-provisioning only"* is superseded in exactly that: asking is permitted; **asking still grants nothing** — authority is written to `user_branch_role` only by a Super Admin's audited approval, and pre-provisioning remains. The form offers four choices, any combination: **«أسجّل نفسي كمستفيدة»** · **«أسجّل أبنائي»** (each child's data, repeatable; the guardian's own data is asked ONLY when no other adult role is ticked — any adult role may attach children, and that role's section already is the same person's data) · **«أسجّل نفسي مع هيئة التدريس والمساعدة في التدريس»** (main and assistant teachers — one role, `teacher`; *assistant* remains a position on a class, R91) · **«أسجّل نفسي مع هيئة الإدارة والمساعدة في الإدارة»** (`admin` or `super_admin` — which, and over which branches, is the approving Super Admin's decision, never the applicant's). Identity is asked once whatever is ticked. The proposal's remaining recommendations stand as drafted until the Owner says otherwise: one notification per decision; a declined role may be asked for again; the ordered memorisation-circle choice is offered from SCHEDULED classes (never a typed list) to a first-time مستفيدة only, and a returning one is placed by the administration.

**§1 as built.** **(a) The request.** `POST /registrations` gains a third shape, `kind: "roles"`: `roles` (one to four of `student · guardian · teaching · administration`), ONE `applicant`, and a section for every role asked for and for no other — `student { branch_id, category_id, first_time, circle_preferences? }` (a date of birth exactly when `student` is asked, R130), `children[]` for `guardian`, `teaching { framing }` (R115 unchanged), `administration { branch_id | null }` — **a preference for where she would serve and nothing else: no field of the request names an administrative role**. The two earlier shapes remain accepted and are normalised into the same write. Nothing is preselected on the form: a box ticked for her is a request she did not make. *(Reversed by Revision 170 §2, at the Owner's word: «أسجّل نفسي كمستفيدة» is chosen by default, in one closed control.)* **(b) Two entities.** `RoleRequest` (one per person and role: `pending → approved | declined`, who decided and when, the decline reason, and `first_time` for `student`) and `CirclePreference` (her ranked حلقات, 1 = most convenient — **a wish, never a seat**). Both go with the person (`ON DELETE CASCADE`), so account deletion and rejection-retention need no new rule. **(c) The circles on offer** — `GET /registration/circle-slots?category_id=&branch_id=`, anonymous like the form: the live weekly classes of the memorisation Subject (by `tracks_quran_progress`, never by name) addressed to a حلقة of the Category's FIRST Level at that branch, each with its days and times, plus the Level's whole-Level classes as information («للجميع» — no choice). It publishes no staff, room or member count and never a «مخفي» class. A Level or branch with no such classes offers nothing and the form asks nothing — «other Levels have no choice» and «أمرشيش later» need no list of exceptions. A preference naming a circle not on offer is refused (`CIRCLE_NOT_OFFERED`). **(d) Each role decided on its own** — `POST /admin/approvals/{id}/roles/{kind}/approve|decline`: `student` is approved by PLACING her (the body carries R66.5's `enrollment`; without it `ENROLLMENT_REQUIRED`); `teaching` grants `teacher` and `administration` grants `admin` or `super_admin` for the scope the APPROVER states (`ROLE_NOT_GRANTABLE_HERE` otherwise; `SUPER_ADMIN_IS_UNSCOPED`); `guardian` grants nothing — `parent` still arrives with her first approved child (R62) — and approving a child directly IS accepting her as a guardian. A decline carries a reason (§5.6), and declining `guardian` rejects her pending child applications with it. **An `administration` request is decided by a Super Admin only**, on either path. Roles are ADDED, never replaced: approving a second role cannot revoke the first. **(e) The account follows its requests:** `pending → active` with the FIRST approval; `rejected` — sessions revoked in the same transaction (R102) — only when every request was declined. An active account with a request still pending stays in the approval queue. **(f) The whole-account decision** (`POST /admin/approvals/{id}/approve|reject`) remains for a registration that asked for one ordinary thing, and keeps its `RoleRequest` in step; for several requests, or a lone `administration` request, it answers `VALIDATION_FAILED` `DECIDE_PER_ROLE` — «approve» has no single meaning there. `GET /admin/approvals` carries `role_requests[]` and `circle_preferences[]` on every registration item. **(g) The self-managed claim (R132) is not one of the roles**: it claims a record that exists. Its entry stays withdrawn from the form (R160 §8) and is reached by `/register?mode=self-managed`. **Not built, and said:** an account that already exists cannot yet ASK for a further role through a form — «a declined role may be asked for again» is, for now, the administration granting it from «المستخدمون».

**§2 — A RECORDER THAT DIES MID-CLASS LOSES NOTHING RECORDED — AND NOTHING AFTER IT.** Revision 167 §5 declared this residual risk; the Owner asked for it to be closed. **(a) Safety segments.** The recorder is asked for TWO outputs of one recording: the final file, uploaded once when the class ends, and the same recording as ten-second HLS segments, each uploaded the moment it is complete, under `<recording-id>.segments/` beside it. One encode feeds both, so a صوت فقط class is now AAC in MP4 (`audio/mp4`, a TD-9 type that also plays on every iPhone, which OGG did not) — HLS is AAC, and the recorder refuses two outputs needing different codecs. Every recording's staging key ends `.mp4` (the CONTAINER's extension; asked for `.m4a` the recorder writes `.m4a.mp4` and reports that back — found by the drill), and the segments folder is derived from the recording's ID, never from «the key minus an extension». **(b) Silence is the fact; the provider's word is not.** Measured on the real stack: a recorder whose container was killed outright was still reported «active» minutes later, with no end in sight. A live recorder uploads a segment every ten seconds, so the newest segment's timestamp is its last sign of life. **(c) The rest of the class.** The classroom shows, after twenty seconds of the ROOM saying nothing is recorded while the platform's row says it is, that the recorder stopped and that what was recorded is safe, and offers «بدء التسجيل» again; the server believes her only when storage agrees (ninety silent seconds), RETIRES the dead recording out of the live states (`processing`, its segments kept; the provider is asked to stop the job first, so a recorder that was alive after all delivers its own complete file, which always wins) and starts a new one. **(d) Assembly.** `session-recording-reconcile` retires a recording still marked live after ten silent minutes (only one that has delivered at least one segment — a recording from before this revision writes none, and its silence says nothing), and on a LATER pass assembles a retired or provider-failed recording whose final file never arrived: the segments are streamed from storage into one static `ffmpeg` (pinned by digest in the API image; REMUX only, nothing re-encoded, no input ever on disk) and written to the recording's own `output_key`. From there it is an ordinary `completed` recording — verified, imported and swept by the unchanged pipeline (R99.13); `session_recording.recovered_from_segments` records how it was obtained and the library item says so, because its last seconds may be missing. **That is the one sanctioned way out of `failed`/`aborted`: the reason for the failure — no file — is no longer a fact.** A failed assembly is said (`ingestion_failure_reason`), keeps every segment, and is tried again; after a successful import the segments are swept with the file. **(e) A recording is as long as the class was.** TD-9's 100 MB (audio) and 500 MB (video) caps bound what a PERSON may upload; applied to the platform's own capture they refused — on import, permanently — every audio class over about 1 h 40 and every video class over about 47 minutes at the measured bitrates. The platform's own recording is bounded at 5 GiB instead. **Cost of a crash: at most the last ten seconds.** Operators gain `ops:reconcile-recordings` (one pass, now) and `ops:recording-segments <id>` (read-only: what storage holds and what the provider says).

**§3 — A LEVEL IS COMPLETED WHEN THE ADMINISTRATION RECORDS IT.** This closes Revision 166's open question (*taken* or *passed*?): **neither.** «إتمام المستوى» is the administration's act — «تسجيل إتمام المستوى», Revision 167 §3 — and nothing else marks a Level completed for now. BR-11's derived reading (every Surah memorised, and an exam of each taken where the Level teaches تفسير) stays exactly as computed, but as the CONDITIONS shown beside that act, never as the verdict: «حفظي» and the مؤطِّرة's Quran screen say «أتمّت المستوى» only where a mark exists (`completion.marked_on`, a Morocco calendar date), «استوفت شروط الإتمام — بانتظار تسجيل الإدارة» where every condition is met and nobody has recorded it, and «لم تُستوفَ شروط الإتمام بعد» otherwise, with the Surahs still to memorise and to be examined on named as before.

**§4 — A SCHEDULING TYPE'S NAME IS THE SUPER ADMIN'S TO CHANGE, AND NOTHING MAY BREAK WHEN SHE DOES.** R110 made the catalogue editable reference data; on 2026-09-21 the Owner renamed «حصة دراسية» to «حصة» and five browser harnesses went blind. Audited: **no application code reads a type's name** — behaviour hangs on `structural_kind` and `attendance_mode`, the name is only composed into titles — and the production seed initialises the catalogue ONCE (a marker, not a name match), so a renamed, re-flagged, reordered or retired type is never brought back by a deployment; that is now pinned by a test. The fragility was the tooling's: harnesses ask the platform for the live catalogue and address a type by what it IS, never by what it is called. **The fresh-install baseline name becomes «حصة»** (R110(2) amended in that one value); an installation that already has a catalogue keeps whatever its Super Admin calls each row.

**§5 — The certificate carries no seal** under the hadith (Owner, 2026-09-21); its foot is the certificate number and the issue date with «إدارة الجمعية».

**Revision 167 (Document Owner decisions — the platform follows Morocco's clock, a recording is never left behind, a recording for a whole Category, «إتمام المستوى» and its certificate, one enrolments dialog, install as an app, and the composed title shown while composing, ratified 2026-09-21):** **SIX BUILT AND VERIFIED; THE SEVENTH (one registration form for several roles) IS A PROPOSAL AWAITING THE OWNER — [`SRS-PROPOSAL-R168.md`](SRS-PROPOSAL-R168.md) — BECAUSE IT REVERSES FOUR STATED RULES.** One migration (`20260922090000_r167_whole_category_content_and_level_completion_mark` — one column, one table, one sequence; nothing dropped or rewritten). Every contract change is additive.

**§1 — «العنوان (يُنشأ تلقائيًا)» is SHOWN in «إضافة عنصر».** Revision 166 §3 removed the typed title of a class and an exam; nothing on the form then said a title would exist, so an author could reasonably type one into «الوصف». The form now shows the composed title, read-only and live, in the place the field used to be, with one sentence saying it is generated and that «الوصف» is for anything more. It is a PREVIEW composed from the same parts in the same order (`composeTitlePreview`); the server still composes the stored and served title and remains the authority (R166 §3 unchanged). An activity and a holiday keep their typed title.

**§2 — THE PLATFORM FOLLOWS THE TIME MOROCCO ACTUALLY OBSERVES, AND LEARNS OF A CHANGE WITHOUT A RELEASE.** Morocco moved to UTC+0 on Sunday 20 September 2026. The host learned it the same day (`tzdata` ships through the operating system's unattended upgrades); the platform did not, because Node converts local time with the ICU zone data compiled into its binary and a container image freezes both that and its own `tzdata` on the day it was built — so every instant computed from a wall-clock time was an hour out, and would have been again at the next decree. **The rule:** one file is the authority — the HOST's `Africa/Casablanca` TZif, bind-mounted read-only over `/usr/share/zoneinfo` in the `api` and `db` containers. The backend parses it itself (`lib/morocco-clock.ts`, RFC 8536), re-reads it when it changes (checked every five minutes), and **nothing in `backend/src` may read the process's local clock** (`getHours`, `new Date(y, m, d…)`, `toLocale…` without a zone) — a CI guard, `check-no-local-clock.sh`. If the file cannot be read the answer falls back to ICU and SAYS so. **`GET /clock` (TD-3, public, cacheable five minutes)** publishes `{ now, zone, utc_offset_minutes, in_force_since, next_change_at, source }`; the browser formats instants with that offset rather than its own possibly outdated zone data, so a phone that has not been updated still shows Morocco's time.
